```json
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Generate Fig 2: Layer-wise Probe Accuracy Curves for Llama-3.1 and Qwen-3.\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nDATA_PATH = Path(__file__).parent / \"layerwise_probe_curves.json\"\nOUT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent / \"figures\" / \"paper\"\nOUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\nwith open(DATA_PATH) as f:\n    data = json.load(f)\n\n# --- helpers ---\nDIR_LABELS = {\n    \"en_en\": r\"EN$\\rightarrow$EN\",\n    \"zh_zh\": r\"ZH$\\rightarrow$ZH\",\n    \"en_zh\": r\"EN$\\rightarrow$ZH\",\n    \"zh_en\": r\"ZH$\\rightarrow$EN\",\n}\nDIR_COLORS = {\n    \"en_en\": \"#2171b5\",\n    \"zh_zh\": \"#e6550d\",\n    \"en_zh\": \"#31a354\",\n    \"zh_en\": \"#de2d26\",\n}\n\ndef get_curve(section, direction):\n    d = section[\"directions\"][direction]\n    layers = sorted(d.keys(), key=int)\n    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), np.array([d[l] for l in layers])\n\ndef get_ci(ci_section, direction):\n    d = ci_section[\"directions\"][direction]\n    layers = sorted(d.keys(), key=int)\n    mean = np.array([d[l][\"mean\"] for l in layers])\n    lo = np.array([d[l][\"ci_lower\"] for l in layers])\n    hi = np.array([d[l][\"ci_upper\"] for l in layers])\n    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), mean, lo, hi\n\n# --- figure: 1×2 layout ---\nfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(7.0, 3.2), sharey=True)\n\nmodels = [\n    (\"Llama-3.1-8B\", \"llama31\", \"llama31_mlp\", None, 32),\n    (\"Qwen3-4B\", \"qwen3\", \"qwen3_mlp\", \"qwen3_bootstrap_ci\", 36),\n]\n\nfor ax, (title, res_key, mlp_key, ci_key, n_layers) in zip(axes, models):\n    for direction in [\"en_en\", \"zh_zh\", \"en_zh\", \"zh_en\"]:\n        c = DIR_COLORS[direction]\n        label = DIR_LABELS[direction]\n\n        # residual stream (solid)\n        lx, ly = get_curve(data[res_key], direction)\n        ax.plot(lx, ly, color=c, linewidth=1.3, label=label + \" (res.)\",\n                zorder=3)\n\n        # MLP (dashed)\n        mx, my = get_curve(data[mlp_key], direction)\n        ax.plot(mx, my, color=c, linewidth=1.1, linestyle=\"--\", alpha=0.7,\n                label=label + \" (MLP)\", zorder=2)\n\n        # bootstrap CI (Qwen only, residual stream)\n        if ci_key:\n            cx, cm, clo, chi = get_ci(data[ci_key], direction)\n            ax.fill_between(cx, clo, chi, color=c, alpha=0.10, zorder=1)\n\n    # peak markers (residual)\n    for direction in [\"en_en\", \"zh_zh\", \"en_zh\", \"zh_en\"]:\n        pk = data[res_key][\"peaks\"][direction]\n        ax.plot(pk[\"peak_layer\"], pk[\"peak_acc\"], color=DIR_COLORS[direction], zorder=5, alpha=0.8,\n                markeredgecolor=\"k\", markeredgewidth=0.3)\n\n    # L13 annotation for Llama (steering causal node)\n    if res_key == matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nDATA_PATH = Path(__file__).parent / \"layerwise_probe_curves.json\"\nOUT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent / \"figures\" / \"paper\"\nOUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\nwith open(DATA_PATH) as f:\n    data = json.load(f)\n\n# --- helpers ---\nDIR_LABELS = {\n    \"en_en\": r\"EN$\\rightarrow$EN\",\n    \"zh_zh\": r\"ZH$\\rightarrow$ZH\",\n    \"en_zh\": r\"EN$\\rightarrow$ZH\",\n    \"zh_en\": r\"ZH$\\rightarrow$EN\",\n}\nDIR_COLORS = {\n    \"en_en\": \"#2171b5\",\n    \"zh_zh\": \"#e6550d\",\n    \"en_zh\": \"#31a354\",\n    \"zh_en\": \"#de2d26\",\n}\n\ndef get_curve(section, direction):\n    d = section[\"directions\"][direction]\n    layers = sorted(d.keys(), key=int)\n    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), np.array([d[l] for l in layers])\n\ndef get_ci(ci_section, direction):\n    d = ci_section[\"directions\"][direction]\n    layers = sorted(d.keys(), key=int)\n    mean = np.array([d[l][\"mean\"] for l in layers])\n    lo = np.array([d[l][\"ci_lower\"] for l in layers])\n    hi = np.array([d[l][\"ci_upper\"] for l in layers])\n    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), mean, lo, hi\n\n# --- figure: 1×2 layout ---\nfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(7.0, 3.2), sharey=True)\n\nmodels = [\n    (\"Llama-3.1-8B\", \"llama31\", \"llama31_mlp\", None, 32),\n    (\"Qwen3-4B\", \"qwen3\", \"qwen3_mlp\", \"qwen3_bootstrap_ci\", 36),\n]\n\nfor ax, (title, res_key, mlp_key, ci_key, n_layers) in zip(axes, models):\n    for direction in [\"en_en\", \"zh_zh\", \"en_zh\", \"zh_en\"]:\n        c = DIR_COLORS[direction]\n        label = DIR_LABELS[direction]\n\n        # residual stream (solid)\n        lx, ly = get_curve(data[res_key], direction)\n        ax.plot(lx, ly, color=c, linewidth=1.3, label=label + \" (res.)\",\n                zorder=3)\n\n        # MLP (dashed)\n        mx, my = get_curve(data[mlp_key], direction)\n        ax.plot(mx, my, color=c, linewidth=1.1, linestyle=\"--\", alpha=0.7,\n                label=label + \" (MLP)\", zorder=2)\n\n        # bootstrap CI (Qwen only, residual stream)\n        if ci_key:\n            cx, cm, clo, chi = get_ci(data[ci_key], direction)\n            ax.fill_between(cx, clo, chi, color=c, alpha=0.10, zorder=1)\n\n    # peak markers (residual)\n    for direction in [\"en_en\", \"zh_zh\", \"en_zh\", \"zh_en\"]:\n        pk = data[res_key][\"peaks\"][direction]\n        ax.plot(pk[\"peak_layer\"], pk[\"peak_acc\"], color=DIR_COLORS[direction], zorder=5, alpha=0.8,\n                markeredgecolor=\"k\", markeredgewidth=0.3)\n\n    # L13 annotation for Llama (steering causal node)\n    if res_key == color=\"grey\", ha=\"center\", va=\"bottom\",\n                    fontstyle=\"italic\")\n\n    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=11, fontweight=\"bold\", pad=6)\n    ax.set_xlabel(\"Layer Index\", <details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01Q7BKq5U9QXyuykXzUaddFN`</summary>
```

```text
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Generate Fig 2: Layer-wise Probe Accuracy Curves for Llama-3.1 and Qwen-3."""
3	
4	import json
5	import numpy as np
6	import matplotlib
7	matplotlib.use('Agg')
8	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
9	from pathlib import Path
10	
11	DATA_PATH = Path(__file__).parent / "layerwise_probe_curves.json"
12	OUT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "figures" / "paper"
13	OUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
14	
15	with open(DATA_PATH) as f:
16	    data = json.load(f)
17	
18	# --- helpers ---
19	DIR_LABELS = {
20	    "en_en": r"EN$\rightarrow$EN",
21	    "zh_zh": r"ZH$\rightarrow$ZH",
22	    "en_zh": r"EN$\rightarrow$ZH",
23	    "zh_en": r"ZH$\rightarrow$EN",
24	}
25	DIR_COLORS = {
26	    "en_en": "#2171b5",
27	    "zh_zh": "#e6550d",
28	    "en_zh": "#31a354",
29	    "zh_en": "#de2d26",
30	}
31	
32	def get_curve(section, direction):
33	    d = section["directions"][direction]
34	    layers = sorted(d.keys(), key=int)
35	    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), np.array([d[l] for l in layers])
36	
37	def get_ci(ci_section, direction):
38	    d = ci_section["directions"][direction]
39	    layers = sorted(d.keys(), key=int)
40	    mean = np.array([d[l]["mean"] for l in layers])
41	    lo = np.array([d[l]["ci_lower"] for l in layers])
42	    hi = np.array([d[l]["ci_upper"] for l in layers])
43	    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), mean, lo, hi
44	
45	# --- figure: 1×2 layout ---
46	fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(7.0, 3.2), sharey=True)
47	
48	models = [
49	    ("Llama-3.1-8B", "llama31", "llama31_mlp", None, 32),
50	    ("Qwen3-4B", "qwen3", "qwen3_mlp", "qwen3_bootstrap_ci", 36),
51	]
52	
53	for ax, (title, res_key, mlp_key, ci_key, n_layers) in zip(axes, models):
54	    for direction in ["en_en", "zh_zh", "en_zh", "zh_en"]:
55	        c = DIR_COLORS[direction]
56	        label = DIR_LABELS[direction]
57	
58	        # residual stream (solid)
59	        lx, ly = get_curve(data[res_key], direction)
60	        ax.plot(lx, ly, color=c, linewidth=1.3, label=label + " (res.)",
61	                zorder=3)
62	
63	        # MLP (dashed)
64	        mx, my = get_curve(data[mlp_key], direction)
65	        ax.plot(mx, my, color=c, linewidth=1.1, linestyle="--", alpha=0.7,
66	                label=label + " (MLP)", zorder=2)
67	
68	        # bootstrap CI (Qwen only, residual stream)
69	        if ci_key:
70	            cx, cm, clo, chi = get_ci(data[ci_key], direction)
71	            ax.fill_between(cx, clo, chi, color=c, alpha=0.10, zorder=1)
72	
73	    # peak markers (residual)
74	    for direction in ["en_en", "zh_zh", "en_zh", "zh_en"]:
75	        pk = data[res_key]["peaks"][direction]
76	        ax.plot(pk["peak_layer"], pk["peak_acc"], marker="*", markersize=7,
77	                color=DIR_COLORS[direction], zorder=5,
78	                markeredgecolor="k", markeredgewidth=0.3)
79	
80	    # peak markers (MLP)
81	    for direction in ["en_en", "zh_zh", "en_zh", "zh_en"]:
82	        pk = data[mlp_key]["peaks"][direction]
83	        ax.plot(pk["peak_layer"], pk["peak_acc"], marker="D", markersize=4,
84	                color=DIR_COLORS[direction], zorder=5, alpha=0.8,
85	                markeredgecolor="k", markeredgewidth=0.3)
86	
87	    # L13 annotation for Llama (steering causal node)
88	    if res_key == "llama31":
89	        ax.axvline(x=13, color="grey", linewidth=0.8, linestyle=":", zorder=1)
90	        ax.annotate("L13\n(steering)", xy=(13, 0.48), fontsize=7,
91	                    color="grey", ha="center", va="bottom",
92	                    fontstyle="italic")
93
```

```json
    "originalFile": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Generate Fig 2: Layer-wise Probe Accuracy Curves for Llama-3.1 and Qwen-3.\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nDATA_PATH = Path(__file__).parent / \"layerwise_probe_curves.json\"\nOUT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent / \"figures\" / \"paper\"\nOUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\nwith open(DATA_PATH) as f:\n    data = json.load(f)\n\n# --- helpers ---\nDIR_LABELS = {\n    \"en_en\": r\"EN$\\rightarrow$EN\",\n    \"zh_zh\": r\"ZH$\\rightarrow$ZH\",\n    \"en_zh\": r\"EN$\\rightarrow$ZH\",\n    \"zh_en\": r\"ZH$\\rightarrow$EN\",\n}\nDIR_COLORS = {\n    \"en_en\": \"#2171b5\",\n    \"zh_zh\": \"#e6550d\",\n    \"en_zh\": \"#31a354\",\n    \"zh_en\": \"#de2d26\",\n}\n\ndef get_curve(section, direction):\n    d = section[\"directions\"][direction]\n    layers = sorted(d.keys(), key=int)\n    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), np.array([d[l] for l in layers])\n\ndef get_ci(ci_section, direction):\n    d = ci_section[\"directions\"][direction]\n    layers = sorted(d.keys(), key=int)\n    mean = np.array([d[l][\"mean\"] for l in layers])\n    lo = np.array([d[l][\"ci_lower\"] for l in layers])\n    hi = np.array([d[l][\"ci_upper\"] for l in layers])\n    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), mean, lo, hi\n\n# --- figure: 1×2 layout ---\nfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(7.0, 3.2), sharey=True)\n\nmodels = [\n    (\"Llama-3.1-8B\", \"llama31\", \"llama31_mlp\", None, 32),\n    (\"Qwen3-4B\", \"qwen3\", \"qwen3_mlp\", \"qwen3_bootstrap_ci\", 36),\n]\n\nfor ax, (title, res_key, mlp_key, ci_key, n_layers) in zip(axes, models):\n    for direction in [\"en_en\", \"zh_zh\", \"en_zh\", \"zh_en\"]:\n        c = DIR_COLORS[direction]\n        label = DIR_LABELS[direction]\n\n        # residual stream (solid)\n        lx, ly = get_curve(data[res_key], direction)\n        ax.plot(lx, ly, color=c, linewidth=1.3, label=label + \" (res.)\",\n                zorder=3)\n\n        # MLP (dashed)\n        mx, my = get_curve(data[mlp_key], direction)\n        ax.plot(mx, my, color=c, linewidth=1.1, linestyle=\"--\", alpha=0.7,\n                label=label + \" (MLP)\", zorder=2)\n\n        # bootstrap CI (Qwen only, residual stream)\n        if ci_key:\n            cx, cm, clo, chi = get_ci(data[ci_key], direction)\n            ax.fill_between(cx, clo, chi, color=c, alpha=0.10, zorder=1)\n\n    # peak markers (residual)\n    for direction in [\"en_en\", \"zh_zh\", \"en_zh\", \"zh_en\"]:\n        pk = data[res_key][\"peaks\"][direction]\n        ax.plot(pk[\"peak_layer\"], pk[\"peak_acc\"], marker=\"*\", markersize=7,\n                color=DIR_COLORS[direction], zorder=5,\n                markeredgecolor=\"k\", markeredgewidth=0.3)\n\n    # peak markers (MLP)\n    for direction in [\"en_en\", \"zh_zh\", \"en_zh\", \"zh_en\"]:\n        pk = data[mlp_key][\"peaks\"][direction]\n        ax.plot(pk[\"peak_layer\"], pk[\"peak_acc\"], marker=\"D\", markersize=4,\n                color=DIR_COLORS[direction], zorder=5, alpha=0.8,\n                markeredgecolor=\"k\", markeredgewidth=0.3)\n\n    # L13 annotation for Llama (steering causal node)\n    if res_key == \"llama31\":\n        ax.axvline(x=13, color=\"grey\", linewidth=0.8, linestyle=\":\", zorder=1)\n        ax.annotate(\"L13\\n(steering)\", xy=(13, 0.48), fontsize=7,\n                    color=\"grey\", ha=\"center\", va=\"bottom\",\n                    fontstyle=\"italic\")\n\n    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=11, fontweight=\"bold\", pad=6)\n    ax.set_xlabel(\"Layer Index\", fontsize=10)\n    ax.set_xlim(-0.5, n_layers - 0.5)\n    ax.set_ylim(0.46, 0.84)\n    ax.tick_params(labelsize=9)\n    ax.spines[\"top\"].set_visible(False)\n    ax.spines[\"right\"].set_visible(False)\n\naxes[0].set_ylabel(\"Balanced Accuracy\", fontsize=10)\n\n# shared legend — deduplicate\nhandles, labels = [], []\nfor direction in [\"en_en\", \"zh_zh\", \"en_zh\", \"zh_en\"]:\n    c = DIR_COLORS[direction]\n    lab = DIR_LABELS[direction]\n    handles.append(plt.Line2D([], [], color=c, lw=1.3, label=lab + \" (res.)\"))\n    handles.append(plt.Line2D([], [], color=c, lw=1.1, ls=\"--\", alpha=0.7,\n                               label=lab + \" (MLP)\"))\nlabels = [h.get_label() for h in handles]\n\n# add peak marker legend entries\nhandles.append(plt.Line2D([], [], marker=\"*\", color=\"grey\", ls=\"\",\n                           markersize=7, markeredgecolor=\"k\",\n                           markeredgewidth=0.3, label=\"Peak (res.)\"))\nhandles.append(plt.Line2D([], [], marker=\"D\", color=\"grey\", ls=\"\",\n                           markersize=4, markeredgecolor=\"k\",\n                           markeredgewidth=0.3, label=\"Peak (MLP)\"))\nlabels = [h.get_label() for h in handles]\n\nfig.legend(handles, labels, loc=\"lower center\", ncol=5, fontsize=7,\n           frameon=False, bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, -0.15), columnspacing=1.2,\n           handletextpad=0.4)\n\nplt.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0.05, 1, 1])\n\n# --- save ---\nfig.savefig(OUT_DIR / \"fig2_layerwise_probes.pdf\",\n            bbox_inches=\"tight\", dpi=300)\nfig.savefig(OUT_DIR / \"fig2_layerwise_probes.png\",\n            bbox_inches=\"tight\", dpi=300)\nprint(f\"Saved to {OUT_DIR / 'fig2_layerwise_probes.pdf'}\")\nprint(f\"Saved to {OUT_DIR / 'fig2_layerwise_probes.png'}\")\nplt.close()\n",
```

```json
    "originalFile": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Generate Fig 2: Layer-wise Probe Accuracy Curves for Llama-3.1 and Qwen-3.\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nDATA_PATH = Path(__file__).parent / \"layerwise_probe_curves.json\"\nOUT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent / \"figures\" / \"paper\"\nOUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\nwith open(DATA_PATH) as f:\n    data = json.load(f)\n\n# --- helpers ---\nDIR_LABELS = {\n    \"en_en\": r\"EN$\\rightarrow$EN\",\n    \"zh_zh\": r\"ZH$\\rightarrow$ZH\",\n    \"en_zh\": r\"EN$\\rightarrow$ZH\",\n    \"zh_en\": r\"ZH$\\rightarrow$EN\",\n}\nDIR_COLORS = {\n    \"en_en\": \"#2171b5\",\n    \"zh_zh\": \"#e6550d\",\n    \"en_zh\": \"#31a354\",\n    \"zh_en\": \"#de2d26\",\n}\n\ndef get_curve(section, direction):\n    d = section[\"directions\"][direction]\n    layers = sorted(d.keys(), key=int)\n    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), np.array([d[l] for l in layers])\n\ndef get_ci(ci_section, direction):\n    d = ci_section[\"directions\"][direction]\n    layers = sorted(d.keys(), key=int)\n    mean = np.array([d[l][\"mean\"] for l in layers])\n    lo = np.array([d[l][\"ci_lower\"] for l in layers])\n    hi = np.array([d[l][\"ci_upper\"] for l in layers])\n    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), mean, lo, hi\n\n# --- figure: 1×2 layout ---\nfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(7.0, 3.4), sharey=True)\n\nmodels = [\n    (\"Llama-3.1-8B\", \"llama31\", \"llama31_mlp\", None, 32),\n    (\"Qwen3-4B\", \"qwen3\", \"qwen3_mlp\", \"qwen3_bootstrap_ci\", 36),\n]\n\nfor ax, (title, res_key, mlp_key, ci_key, n_layers) in zip(axes, models):\n    for direction in [\"en_en\", \"zh_zh\", \"en_zh\", \"zh_en\"]:\n        c = DIR_COLORS[direction]\n\n        # residual stream (solid)\n        lx, ly = get_curve(data[res_key], direction)\n        ax.plot(lx, ly, color=c, linewidth=1.4, zorder=3)\n\n        # MLP (dashed)\n        mx, my = get_curve(data[mlp_key], direction)\n        ax.plot(mx, my, color=c, linewidth=1.0, linestyle=\"--\", alpha=0.65,\n                zorder=2)\n\n        # bootstrap CI (Qwen only, residual stream)\n        if ci_key:\n            cx, cm, clo, chi = get_ci(data[ci_key], direction)\n            ax.fill_between(cx, clo, chi, color=c, alpha=0.08, zorder=1)\n\n    # peak markers (residual — star)\n    for direction in [\"en_en\", \"zh_zh\", \"en_zh\", \"zh_en\"]:\n        pk = data[res_key][\"peaks\"][direction]\n        ax.plot(pk[\"peak_layer\"], pk[\"peak_acc\"], marker=\"*\", markersize=8,\n                color=DIR_COLORS[direction], zorder=5,\n                markeredgecolor=\"k\", markeredgewidth=0.4)\n\n    # peak markers (MLP — diamond)\n    for direction in [\"en_en\", \"zh_zh\", \"en_zh\", \"zh_en\"]:\n        pk = data[mlp_key][\"peaks\"][direction]\n        ax.plot(pk[\"peak_layer\"], pk[\"peak_acc\"], marker=\"D\", markersize=4.5,\n                color=DIR_COLORS[direction], zorder=5, alpha=0.85,\n                markeredgecolor=\"k\", markeredgewidth=0.4)\n\n    # L13 annotation for Llama (steering causal node)\n    if res_key == \"llama31\":\n        ax.axvline(x=13, color=\"#555555\", linewidth=0.9, linestyle=\":\",\n                   zorder=1, alpha=0.7)\n        ax.annotate(\"L13\\n(steering)\", xy=(13, 0.47), fontsize=7.5,\n                    color=\"#555555\", ha=\"center\", va=\"bottom\",\n                    fontstyle=\"italic\")\n\n    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=11, fontweight=\"bold\", pad=6)\n    ax.set_xlabel(\"Layer Index\", fontsize=10)\n    ax.set_xlim(-0.5, n_layers - 0.5)\n    ax.set_ylim(0.45, 0.84)\n    ax.tick_params(labelsize=9)\n    ax.spines[\"top\"].set_visible(False)\n    ax.spines[\"right\"].set_visible(False)\n\naxes[0].set_ylabel(\"Balanced Accuracy\", fontsize=10)\n\n# compact legend: 4 direction colors + line-style explanation\nhandles = []\nfor direction in [\"en_en\", \"zh_zh\", \"en_zh\", \"zh_en\"]:\n    handles.append(plt.Line2D([], [], color=DIR_COLORS[direction], lw=1.5,\n                               label=DIR_LABELS[direction]))\nhandles.append(plt.Line2D([], [], color=\"grey\", lw=1.3, ls=\"-\",\n                           label=\"Residual stream\"))\nhandles.append(plt.Line2D([], [], color=\"grey\", lw=1.0, ls=\"--\", alpha=0.65,\n                           label=\"MLP output\"))\nhandles.append(plt.Line2D([], [], marker=\"*\", color=\"grey\", ls=\"\",\n                           markersize=7, markeredgecolor=\"k\",\n                           markeredgewidth=0.3, label=r\"$\\bigstar$ Peak (res.)\"))\nhandles.append(plt.Line2D([], [], marker=\"D\", color=\"grey\", ls=\"\",\n                           markersize=4, markeredgecolor=\"k\",\n                           markeredgewidth=0.3, label=r\"$\\Diamond$ Peak (MLP)\"))\nlabels = [h.get_label() for h in handles]\n\nfig.legend(handles, labels, loc=\"lower center\", ncol=4, fontsize=7.5,\n           frameon=False, bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, -0.12), columnspacing=1.5,\n           handletextpad=0.5)\n\nplt.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0.04, 1, 1])\n\n# --- save ---\nfig.savefig(OUT_DIR / \"fig2_layerwise_probes.pdf\",\n            bbox_inches=\"tight\", dpi=300)\nfig.savefig(OUT_DIR / \"fig2_layerwise_probes.png\",\n            bbox_inches=\"tight\", dpi=300)\nprint(f\"Saved to {OUT_DIR / 'fig2_layerwise_probes.pdf'}\")\nprint(f\"Saved to {OUT_DIR /
```
